Entry 1
Category: Asia
Country: India
Continent: Asia
Capital: New Delhi
Currency: Indian Rupee
Government: Federal Parliamentary Republic
Key Points:
- Important country in world geography.
- Commonly covered in geography and general knowledge examinations.
Example: New Delhi is the capital city of India.
Related Concepts:
Capitals, Continents, Currencies, Governments, Geography
